Unresolved readings

George Douglas Papers annotated transcription · Version 1.1

Each item links to its transcript paragraph. The TSV copy retains contextual proposals and review evidence. Original uncertainty IDs remain stable; retired IDs appear in the revision ledger. This register records uncertainty in transcription, not the truth of a writer’s statements.
